# Words this project moved off the valid list.
#
# They are here because no dictionary we could reach recognised
# them on the day they were demoted -- which is not the same as
# their not being words. The nightly run reserves part of each
# night for this queue and ignores the pre-filter that would
# otherwise hide the longer ones, so every one of them gets
# looked at again. See corrections/README.md.
#
# Demoted 2026-08-14 by stage 4: gerund plurals, verb forms and agent-noun plurals. The 28,605 synthetic plurals were deliberately left alone.
